# WFC Retired Duplicate Nx Cache

All-2 full source custody closed exact with all fifteen observations before this deletion; no WFC runner was live. Removed only the abandoned owner-local grid2d Nx workspace data directory. It contains regenerable tooling caches; all command outputs, runtime receipts, lease metadata, fixture/schema inputs and original source journals remain. The existing 2d graph cache is now the explicitly authored common successor coordinate. No shared Native6 or peer context was removed.
